When you switch your identity from the little seen self, or object self, to the true Seer or Real Self, all you sense is a vast Freedom and Release, an ocean of Openness, a vast clear field or Space or Clearing in which all objects, inside and outside, are arising moment to moment. You are this vast, open, empty Clearing or Space or Spaciousness in which everything is arising, moment to moment to moment. You are what the Awakened have called neti, neti, which means “not this, not that.”5 I am not this, not that, not any object or thing that can be seen, but rather the vast Opening or Clearing in which all those objects or things are arising, now and now and now.

This vast opening is an ongoing sense of pure I AMness. It is an ever-present awareness, whether you realize it yet or not (and is present even in deep dreamless sleep). I AMness is the one constant experience you always have: it is ever-present. You probably can’t remember what you were doing on this day a month ago—but you can be sure I AMness was present. You likely can’t remember what you were doing a decade ago, but I AMness was present. You probably can’t remember what you were doing a century ago, a millennium ago, but I AMness was there. And that simply means, not that I AMness is literally everlasting, but that it is timeless, it lives in the present timeless moment, the pure Now-moment, and never enters the stream of time, and so all time is present for it. The Witness is aware of time, and thus is itself Free of time—neti, neti. And we saw that the real meaning of “eternity” is not everlasting time, but a moment free of time, the timeless Now-moment or pure Present. We quoted Wittgenstein: “If we take eternity to mean not everlasting temporal duration but timelessness, then eternal life belongs to those who live in the Present.” And since you are always already grounded in pure I AMness, you are always already, realize it or not, living in eternity (whenever you rest as pure I AMness).

(How is the Witness only aware of the Now-moment, without making any effort whatsoever to do so? Well, think of any past moment you wish. You think that past is real, but notice that all you are directly aware of is just a memory of that past, and that memory is arising only in the present Now-moment. That thought of the past is itself actually occurring Now. And when

that past moment was actually occurring—when it was really real—it was a present Now-moment, too. Likewise, think of any future event you like. That thought, as well, is a present thought, a Now-moment. And when that future becomes real, or actually occurs, it, too, will actually be a present Now-moment. As Erwin Schrödinger, cofounder of modern quantum mechanics, said, “The present is the only thing that has no end.”6 There is only and actually nothing but the Now-moment as it unfolds, now and now and now— and this Now-moment is the only thing that is ever actually entering your awareness. The Witness is always and only aware of the Now-moment, which means, as we’ll see, that the Witness is living in eternity. Some spiritual practices would have us practice “living in the now” or “be here now” by focusing our attention on just the present moment, deliberately excluding thoughts of past and future. But that gives us just the narrow present, the passing present, split from the past and the future; the real, timeless Now is not something you have to work to achieve, it’s something that you can’t avoid in any case—it’s all you’re ever aware of—and it includes and embraces thoughts of past and present and future all in its eternal, timeless, all-time Present.)

Recall the Zen koan that says, “Show me your Original Face, the face you had before your parents were born.” A Self you had before your parents were born? Yes, and Zen means this seriously and literally—it’s not a metaphor or a symbol, but a direct experiential fact, if you look at it correctly. Your Original Face is your True Self, the Witness, the Real Seer, and it exists before your parents were born simply because, again, it never enters the stream of time. It exists in the timeless Now. It is aware of time passing, but is not itself in time. It is timeless, which is the real meaning of eternal. And that is what the Witness does—it is always perfectly Witnessing all the objects and events and things that are arising in this vast, empty, open clearing that is Now.

Christ said, “Before Abraham was, I AM.” And that’s exactly right. God said, “I AM that I AM”—right on the mark. That I AMness—the immediate and direct feeling of pure I AMness—not I AM this or I AM that, but just the immediate, direct, pure feeling of I AMness itself—is timeless, eternal, the Original Face you had before your parents were born, before the universe was born, before time itself was born—right Now! (Exactly the I AMness that you are feeling in this very moment—you’re aware of that, yes? Just as it is before it is anything else? That’s it! That’s always it! Always has been,

always will be. . . .) So as you practice mindfulness of any object, you are actually resting in

nothing but the pure Witness, and you are witnessing the present timeless Now as events come and go through it, as they come and go through you, through the Witnessing awareness that you are. That Witnessing awareness is the spacious, groundless Ground of all that exists, and all that did exist, and all that will exist—all arising in this vast, open, empty Clearing—timeless Present after timeless Present after timeless Present—an ongoing timeless Presence as the timeless Present. And this very sense of pure present I AMness—this simple immediate feeling of Being—again, you are aware of that right now, yes? Just this simple present feeling of Being?—this is your True Self, your Real Self, beyond any temporal or finite ripple of this or that, but the Ground, the Source, the Witness of all of that.

So, resting in this pure sense of Witnessing awareness, which is no object whatsoever, take it to its conclusion, and radically, radically, let go of all of the objects we have been identifying and objectifying. You are not, in your real Core, any particular level or hidden map whatsoever—zero, none—you are truly neti, neti. Every single thing and event and object you are aware of now is simply part of the limited, manifest, object world—and you are Free, radically Free, from all of it. Rest in that state of pure infinite Freedom—feel it as radiant spacious Freedom (with no limits or boundaries whatsoever)— which, no matter what object arises, within or without, you immediately dis-identify with it—I am not this, I am not that—and simply let it arise as it wishes to arise, in the pure clear Mirror-Mind that you are, with no resistance, no grasping, no contracting, just a pure simple Reflection of All That Is. I am the pure Witness of this and that, the pure Observing Self, the True Seer, the vast clearing or open Space in which all of this arises, and which is identified with none of it.

With this practice you are making every finite subject into an object of Infinite or Absolute Subjectivity (which is beyond both subjects and objects, and is the simple Clearing or Space in which all objects and subjects arise). But to make all subjects anywhere into objects of the True Self or Real Seer is to be free, radically and totally free, from all of them.

Forever and truly free The single witness of all things. But if you see yourself as separate,

Then you are bound.7

And that is what we have been doing—binding ourselves by identifying our True Self or pure Seer with something that can be seen, dragging our infinite Real Condition down and into the finite, limited, partial, fragmented, and suffering realm, hence opening ourselves to endless suffering and dismay, with life ending up an endless series of unsatisfying, painful, unfulfilling moments. As Thoreau famously wrote in Walden, the mass of men and women lead “lives of quiet desperation”—and in the words of Oliver Wendell Holmes, “die with all their music in them.” But to be free of those little finite objects is to be Free, period.

That is radical Freedom, Freedom from all objects anywhere, Freedom from the entire universe, Freedom from all of manifestation itself in any realm. It is the Freedom of your Original Face, the total freedom of Spirit, the radical Freedom of I AMness, which is pure I AMness before it identifies with anything—not I AM this tall, I AM this weight, I AM at this job, I AM this name, I AM this body, and so on—just the pure I AMness before it is any of that, the simple feeling of I AMness itself before it is anything else at all, your True Self, not your object self.

But what happens, at each stage of Growing Up, is that you will identify your small, conventional, finite, subjective self with the particular level or hidden map of that stage. And the Witness will then look through that small self and its map at the world, using that map to interpret what it sees. Not even the Witness knows what that hidden map is—remember, you can’t see any of them by looking within, by simply giving them awareness or Witnessing them. The Witness will continue to look at, and interpret, the world from the map of whatever level you are at. Notice that when the Witness is aware of a particular small, subjective self, and sees it as an object, it still doesn’t see the hidden form or the hidden map of that little self. It just sees all the words and sentences and letters and paragraphs of that little self, but not the grammar holding it all together—that can’t be seen merely by looking or Witnessing. So this is why the path of Waking Up can proceed, state by state by state, but never be aware of any of the levels or stages of Growing Up that it happens to be going through. The hidden maps remain hidden even to the Witness, to the True Self. It doesn’t even know that it is interpreting the world in only one of 7 or 8 different ways of doing so. It simply does so, since that is what is showing up Now in Awareness, and is

none the wiser to the hidden map that it is unknowingly using. That is why you can practice mindfulness all you want, and it still won’t

give you a higher level of Growing Up, because the hidden map never becomes an object of mindfulness, because it is indeed completely hidden—it no more becomes an object of mindfulness than the rules of grammar will become an object. No meditation system anywhere in the world shows us the hidden rules of grammar, and no meditator anywhere experiences them consciously. Same with the hidden maps. Both hidden maps and grammar remain unconscious.

But this “unconsciousness” is different from the typical, common form of the unconscious, where the self represses, denies, dissociates, or dis-owns something that it finds disturbing and banishes it to the basement of the unconscious. You’re not unaware or unconscious of these hidden maps because you are repressing them, or avoiding them, or don’t like them, or dis-own them, but simply because you don’t know they’re there. If you learn about them, and know they are there, and actively look for them, they will rather quickly and easily show up in awareness. But if not, they remain unknown, hidden, unconscious—although fully active and fully working to interpret your world.

Unless, to repeat the central point, you know specifically to look for them, unless you specifically have an overview of the levels of hidden maps in your own system—unless you have something like Integral Theory to alert you to these hidden maps, so that you can indeed make them an intentional object of mindfulness, thus making those hidden subjects into conscious objects, and therefore dis-identifying with them, letting go of them, which then allows the next-higher level of Growing Up to emerge, with its next-higher conventional self with its hidden map, through which the Witness will now view and interpret the world. If you don’t know about those, then Witness all you want, and you’ll never see them. They will remain unconscious but very present and active, and your Witness will continue to look through that hidden map at the world, even if you become fully awakened to, and enlightened to, that Witnessing awareness.

But if you know that map is there, and you know its basic characteristics, then you can focus on those, making those hidden subjects objects, and thus, once again, transcending them, letting them go, and moving up.

That’s what Integral Mindfulness is all about. Combining a knowledge of the levels of Growing Up with a practice of the states of Waking Up, thus

reaching the highest level of Growing Up and the highest state of Waking Up at this stage in evolution, a full and complete and more total Enlightenment.

This implies that as evolution continues, Enlightenment will continue to evolve as well—and that’s right, or half right. Using one of the common, traditional definitions of Enlightenment as “the conscious union of Emptiness and all Form,” Emptiness itself does not evolve—it has no moving parts, no finite manifestations, nothing to evolve, but is timeless, unchanging Being (metaphorically), and thus the Freedom that it offers is likewise timeless and unchanging—but the world of Form most definitely evolves as the world of Becoming, with each major stage of evolution transcending-and-including its predecessor—quarks to atoms to molecules to cells to organisms—and thus each level of evolution becomes Fuller and Fuller, consisting of more and more Form, more and more Wholeness in the levels of the manifest realm (e.g., cells transcend and include molecules, and thus are Fuller—are more complex and thus have more Form, and hence more Wholeness—which transcends and includes the Wholeness of a molecule—and so on).

So a sage’s Enlightenment today is no Freer than a sage’s Enlightenment 2,000 years ago—because the same unchanging, timeless Emptiness gives the same Freedom—but today’s sage, if fully Grown, has two or three more levels or structures of reality that he or she today is one with. Instead of being, say, amber or orange, today’s sage, to be fully Enlightened, would have to be at least at a turquoise Integral level of Form, and thus his or her Enlightenment is Fuller, has more Form, is more Inclusive, than the sage’s of 2,000 years ago, as evolution continues its endless “creative advance into novelty,”8 which transcends and includes its predecessors, thus creating greater and greater and greater manifest Wholes, which today’s sage must be one with in nondual unity consciousness.

Thus, using both Growing Up and Waking Up as metrics, we redefine Enlightenment as “being one with both the highest structure/level and the highest state to emerge in evolution at any given time.” Thus, Enlightenment during amber was one with an amber world, Enlightenment during orange was one with an orange world, Enlightenment during green was one with a green world, and Enlightenment during integral is one with an integral world, which is the leading edge of evolution today and essentially the highest—and thus Fullest Form—available anywhere in the world. Of course, unity consciousness is available at all of those junior levels, but each lower unity is less Full, less truly Whole, than its senior successor, and thus is “less

Enlightened” than its successor’s version. But it was “fully Enlightened” for its time, when there were no higher levels yet introduced by evolution, and thus its level was the highest level, the greatest manifest Wholeness, the largest Form, in the entire world at that time, and thus a highest-state nondual unity with it constituted that era’s Enlightenment. With evolution, Enlightenments thus become Fuller, but not Freer. (Personally, I’ve always liked that formula, because it gives ancient sages their due, but also gives evolution its due, both of which seem important.)

Turiyatita: Ultimate Nondual Unity Consciousness

Speaking of that highest state of Waking Up—namely, nondual unity consciousness—let’s now move to that and some exercises for directly glimpsing it.

The ultimate Freedom of the pure Witness rests on an even deeper truth— you are not any one of those objects that are arising moment to moment, because in your very deepest Nature you are ALL of them. That’s right, you are all objects and subjects and things and events arising anywhere and everywhere. You are actually in an ever-present state of unity consciousness, where your True Witness is one with everything that it Witnesses, whether gross, subtle, or causal. So let’s experience that. . . . Begin by sitting comfortably again, relax the mind, and then pick an object arising in your awareness right now—a tree, a table, a cup, a computer, a building, any object like that. Now get in the stance of the Witness. That is, look at this object from the stance of the pure Seer, the pure Observing Self. And that means you go through the process of neti, neti—“I have sensations, but am not those sensations; I have feelings, but am not those feelings; I have thoughts, but am not those thoughts. I am none of those. I am their pure Witness, the pure Observing Awareness.”

Now, while looking at the object, allow the Observing Self, the Looker, to dissolve. Just let go of any sense of being a watcher, by simply focusing entirely on the object itself. Let the object arise in your awareness as if it existed by itself; let the feeling of the object push out any feeling of a watcher by just existing all on its own, as totally self-arising and self-existing. You don’t see it; you don’t exist. There is just the object arising in your empty, clear field of awareness, without a Looker—just the object arising by itself, as itself—just the object. Douglas Harding called this the headless state in his

spiritual classic On Having No Head.9 It works like this. As the awareness of the object continues to arise, notice that it is arising directly on your shoulders, exactly where you thought your head was. That is, you can’t really see your head sitting there on your shoulders; where you thought your head was, you can only see the object sitting there right on your shoulders. In terms of your direct experience—what you are actually aware of—you can’t see your head, there are just two fleshy blobs arising a little bit in front—your nose—but in your direct awareness, your head is a just a big empty space, an opening or clearing, a pure Emptiness itself. But look closely, and the object is actually right there directly in that empty Space—the object is sitting right there in that empty Space exactly where you thought your head was. That’s all you are actually directly aware of. . . .

There is no “inside your face” in here and “outside your face” out there— everything out there is actually arising in here, inside your face, on your shoulders where your head used to be. In fact, the world out there—the entire universe—is arising on this side of your face, is arising inside of you, right here where your head used to be. Your head disappears into the entire Space or Clearing in which all objects are arising right now, and now, and now. You are one with everything that is arising moment to moment. You don’t see the object, you are the object. You don’t see the mountain, you are the mountain —it’s arising right here, where your head once was. You don’t feel the earth, you are the earth. You don’t see the clouds, you are the clouds. You are the entire manifest world, arising moment to moment to moment on this side of your face right where your head once was, arising within you—and hence the feeling of “you” expands to ALL of that space, that huge space which was once “out there” but is now totally one with you—you ARE that space, that spaciousness, that open clearing. The feeling of that space—in which everything is arising—and the feeling of you—which is this empty, open, headless feeling—are one and the same feeling, one and the same thing. That space is you; you are that space—and everything exists in it, everything is arising within it, that headless you/space or spaciousness. You are not in this room, this room is in you—in your spacious nondual awareness, in this open clearing where your head once was.

Chögyam Trungpa Rinpoche once described what Enlightenment felt like (not its intellectual definition, but what it actually felt like). He said, “The sky turns into a blue pancake and drops on our head.”10 That’s sort of funny—but that’s exactly it. The sky—which used to be “out there” and “separate” from

you—turned into something that has “dropped” on your head—that is now directly touching you, directly one with you—it is actually existing right where you thought your head was. It’s a big blue expanse that is existing right on your headless shoulders, and you are one with it.

This feeling of headless oneness with everything that is arising is a glimmer of true unity consciousness. It’s a union of the Emptiness of your head—which you can’t actually see in your visual field at all; it’s just a clear open space—and it’s one with the Form of everything arising. That’s unity consciousness. In this unity state, you will experience both perfect Freedom —because you are not identified with any single thing or event that is arising, so you are Free from each and every one of them—but you will also experience perfect Fullness, because although you are not identified with any one item alone, you are identified with all of them; you feel that you ARE the entire universe arising on this side of your face, arising within you, where your head used to be. You can taste the sky, literally taste the sky, it is that close—there’s no separation between the Seer and the seen—they literally touch, they are One Taste, right here where your head once was. Looking at the Pacific Ocean, you can pick it up with your fingers and just hold it between them, it’s that easy, that close, right here.

And remember, even though you have this unity awareness, you will still be at a particular level of development—red magic, amber mythic, orange rational, green pluralistic, turquoise integral—and so you will continue to interpret events as they unfold in terms of the hidden map that you have, the level that you’re at. You will fully experience this unity state, but it will still be interpreted in a mythic or pluralistic or integral (etc.) fashion. And you won’t experience that hidden map. Unless you know to look for it, you will be completely unaware of that level, unaware of the fact that it is governing how you see and interpret this experience.

And you can have this headless unity experience at virtually any level— magic, mythic, rational, pluralistic, integral—and again, you won’t be aware that’s happening at all. That is why every one of the meditation systems worldwide, even though they were perfectly aware of this unity state, were not aware of any of the hidden map-levels, and so included none of them in their maps of meditation or their theories of reality.

But if we put both of these together—structures of Growing Up with states of Waking Up—we get something like what is depicted in figure 2.2, known as the “Wilber-Combs Lattice” (after myself and Dr. Allan Combs, who

independently came upon essentially the same idea). Up the vertical axis there are the structures of consciousness in the path of Growing Up, taken from any reputable developmental model you wish (with all of them highlighting, as we have noted, the same basic 6-to-8 levels of development; in this figure, the vertical axis has the basic 7 levels as given by Integral Theory that we just described, which are summary generic descriptions of these rainbow levels of altitude development). And then across the top are the major 4–5 states of consciousness, which, as we have seen, all complete or full paths of Waking Up tend to emphasize in the order given, from left to right. (These states can also be focused on separately, to yield: as a unity with the total gross realm, Nature mysticism; a unity with the subtle realm, a Deity mysticism; a unity with the formless causal/Witness realm, a Formless or Abyss mysticism; and a unity with the ultimate nondual realm, a Nondual or pure Unity mysticism. These are also listed with each major state in the top row.)

Figure 2.2. The Wilber-Combs Lattice. (To view this figure in a larger format, visit www.shambhala.com/integralmeditation.)

And the point, we have seen time and again, is to aim for the highest state experienced via the highest structure that has emerged at any given time in history. Today, this is basically the nondual state and the Integral structure (standing then on the edge of the Super-Integral). What’s so crucial about both of these sequences—Growing Up and Waking Up—is that they are developmental realms—they each grow and develop and evolve through a generally world-recognized series of stages, which can themselves be practiced and exercised. Research continues to indicate that, although stages cannot be skipped or bypassed, they can be accelerated—and one of the first and most basic ways to do this is to simply learn any general overview or summary of the stages in each of these developmental axes. Research shows that somebody who does nothing more than learn the rudimentary basics of any good developmental map or model, for example, will develop through the basic 6-to-8 levels in a more rapid and accelerated fashion—these developmental maps (including AQAL—“all quadrants, all levels, all lines, all states, all types”—especially, since it embraces all the major developmental sequences that humans have open to them) are psychoactive. Simply learning them puts them into play, activates their various levels and realms, and quickens development through them. To a large degree, we can make the future that is given to us.

The “Conveyor Belt” of Humanity’s Transformation

One of the things that the discovery of the 6-to-8 major stages of Growing Up —including the stages of spiritual Growing Up—alerted us to is the fact that there are various individuals at virtually every single stage of spiritual development (i.e., spiritual intelligence) that exists. This is true in every major religious Tradition; and here we are talking about spiritual intelligence in the Growing Up sequence, not direct spiritual experience in the Waking Up sequence. As we have seen, these are the two major types of spiritual involvement we humans have available to us, owing to the two major types of development (Growing Up and Waking Up).

So there is spiritual Growing Up, through the 6-to-8 major structures of development, and there is spiritual Waking Up, through the 4–5 major states

of development. We will explain this fully in a moment, but we can say, for now, that we have seen two sequences of development: one through the 6-to-8 major structures of development, up to Integral and Super-Integral, and one through the 4–5 major states of development, up to nondual unity. And now, simply realize that there can be a spiritual involvement in each of those sequences—the Waking Up sequence is fairly obvious, since it is the core of the world’s great contemplative and meditative spiritual traditions; but recent research has demonstrated that one will interpret one’s spiritual realizations through whatever structure-stage one is at in the Growing Up process. As we will see, this adds an entirely new and deeply important dimension to spiritual involvement. So in that spiritual dimension of Growing Up through 6-to-8 structure-stages, the point is that we have people at every one of those major 6-to-8 stages in the spiritual line: we have people, for example, at magic Christianity, mythic-literal Christianity, rational Christianity, pluralistic Christianity, and beginning integral Christianity (and likewise people in Hinduism, Buddhism, Taoism, Islam, Judaism, etc., at each of those structure-stages).

The theologian James W. Fowler conducted pioneering research on the stages of faith (i.e., the structure-stages of spiritual intelligence). He found that his research subjects went through around 6 or 7 major stages, which he identified and named (and they are all direct variations on archaic, magic, magic-mythic, mythic, rational, pluralistic, and integral—in the spiritual line, of course, which is what we would expect, since spiritual intelligence, being one of the multiple intelligences, would be expected to go through the same 6-to-8 major levels of development as all the other lines do, and Fowler’s research strongly confirmed exactly that conclusion). Dustin DiPerna, one of the most gifted of the current AQAL Integral theorists, sets out to supply extra evidence (and novel applications) for some of the key tenets of Integral Spirituality, including the 4 major vectors of development (structures and structure-stages, or Views; states and state-stages, or Vantage Points).11 He uses Magic, Mythic, Rational, Pluralistic, and Integral-level Views; and gross, subtle, causal, witnessing, and nondual state-realms (and their Vantage Points); and he gives examples that today actually exist at all 5 stages/levels in Christianity, Islam, Hinduism, and Buddhism.

What this means, as I first pointed out in Integral Spirituality,12 is that religion, in fact, is in a position to act as a “conveyor belt” of transformation for humanity on the whole. It is virtually the only human discipline where

there are adult humans at all 6-to-8 major levels of development (in at least some lines). In the natural sciences, for example, virtually all of them are pure rational-stage productions. Science can’t pick people up at magic and help them transform to mythic and then to rational and then to higher stages —it itself doesn’t cover that ground at all. But religion does. And you can find exponents of every single one of those major levels (magic, mythic, rational, pluralistic, integral) in every great religious Tradition everywhere in the world. Christianity, for example: its magic and mythic elements are well known, and its fundamentalist branches believe that those myths are absolutely and unerringly true, the literal word of God. During the rational Enlightenment, the great pioneering scientists were almost all rational Christians, or Deists—as was, for example, the more recent Jesus Seminar of the late 20th century, a group studying the historicity of Jesus. Recently, we have the retired Episcopal bishop John Selby Spong writing about Christianity from the pluralistic postmodern stage. Others have written beautifully on an Integral Christianity; see, for example, Paul Smith’s book of that title.

Thus, what does research into these two basic forms of spiritual involvement that are available to humans—states of consciousness in Waking Up and structures of consciousness in Growing Up—have to tell us when all is said and done? There are at least two major points, if we want anything near an inclusive stance.

The first major point: Every Tradition, if it hasn’t already, should introduce practices—or at least make them available—from their own branches of Waking Up. Virtually all Traditions have some of these schools of “esoteric,” or “inner” teaching, which use meditation or contemplation to move an individual into a deeper and deeper connection with the Divine, resulting in various higher states, and in many cases in the highest state itself, some version of the Supreme Identity—a direct realization and experience, by the individual, of the ultimate Ground of all Being, leading to his or her own Great Liberation. (And on the rare occasions that a particular religious school doesn’t have much of a contemplative branch available to it, it can borrow one either from other schools of its Tradition or, if necessary, even from other Traditions. But being entirely without a dimension of Waking Up is simply unacceptable—a hard but totally justified judgment.) In many ways this is the whole point of any form of spirituality—to serve as a direct introduction to the living, vital, conscious, groundless Ground of Being and True Nature and

Real Self of every sentient being, a Supreme Identity that virtually every major Tradition of the world takes to be the summum bonum of the human condition—the “greatest good.”

But wait. There is a magic Enlightenment, a mythic Enlightenment, a rational Enlightenment, and so on. And if we use, as an example, a typical definition of Enlightenment given by one of the Eastern traditions—“the realization of the union of Emptiness and Form” (or of the formless Godhead and the individual)—it turns out that the Waking Up Enlightenment can be experienced at each of the 6-to-8 stages of Growing Up. And that’s the point: virtually any state-stage of Waking Up—including its highest stage, or nondual Enlightenment—can be experienced at virtually any structure-stage of Growing Up, and that experience will be interpreted according to the terms of the structure-stage—mythic, rational, pluralistic, etc.—having the state-stage experience—subtle, causal, nondual, etc. So the Enlightenment that occurs at any structure-stage will indeed be a union of Emptiness and Form; but although the Emptiness component will be essentially the same at every stage of Growing Up (since it is totally “formless and “unqualifiable”), the Form component (which undergoes development and evolution) will be quite different from stage to stage. At mythic, for example, the individual would discover a union or oneness with the entire world of Form as it exists for that person at amber. But that person’s world only includes Form up to the mythic level. There are, over his or her head, the entire levels of the rational world, the pluralistic world, and the integral world—real worlds, with real structures and real phenomena that the mythic individual is not—and cannot be—one with, because they don’t even exist in his or her awareness; and you cannot be one with that which doesn’t exist for you in any way. So the “unity consciousness” of the mythic individual is indeed a “unity,” but it is not a complete, full unity. It is partial, limited, incomplete, fractional.

Thus, the second major point: Make sure each Tradition is fully on board with the “conveyor belt” for its own teachings, helping individuals not only to Wake Up but also to Grow Up all the way to Integral levels of their spirituality, so that their Awakening to unity consciousness, if and when it occurs, will be a real, true, full unity, including all the worlds—i.e., all the stages of Form—that have emerged and evolved to date. And this also applies directly to the creation and existence of societies that are truly—actually —inclusive, not just as a stated ideal, but as a direct and real human actuality. As developmental research strongly demonstrates, any 1st-tier stage is, by its

very nature, incapable of a true inclusiveness—it simply cannot cognize the requisite complexity or its unification. But the integral or 2nd-tier stages—for the first time in history—bring the real possibility of a genuine inclusiveness, and the mental capacity and fullness to actually put it into practice. Educational and political systems should at least take this real developmental fact into consideration. If it’s developmental goals you want (as in the UN’s “Sustainable Development Goals”), how about starting with development itself as a goal?

And this second point gives rise to the new and updated definition of Enlightenment that we have been using: being one with both the highest state and the highest structure that have evolved at that time in history. Today, that would be the nondual state and the integral structure. In 2000 B.C.E., it would have been the causal state and the mythic structure. But a person who, at that time, was one with that causal state and mythic structure would indeed be one with the entire universe that had emerged at that point—their “unity consciousness” could not have been any more unified, and thus they were Enlightened within the conditions of that point in evolution. But today, evolution (which some regard as “Spirit-in-action”) has indeed moved on, in its relentless “creative advance into novelty,” and today, to be fully one and truly whole, requires a nondual state and an integral structure (and thus so does a full Enlightenment).

Thus, according to our second major point, with the institutionalization of a “conveyor belt,” the main tenets of any particular Tradition are set forth in the language and terms of each of the major structure-stages of spiritual Growing Up, so that somebody could start their young, childhood spiritual development with a magical presentation of their religion; then, during middle school, move to a mythic presentation; then in adolescence, a rational perspective; then—if they continue growing—in young adulthood, a pluralistic view; and finally, if growth fully matures, an integral version of their Tradition. Combined with that Tradition’s path of Waking Up, this would involve the most complete, the most inclusive, the most all-pervading spiritual involvement a human could possibly attain—the highest state and the highest structure made available to date by evolution, by Spirit-in-action —and mark the utter fulfillment of the very best and very brightest for which any sentient being might possibly hope.

“More Whole” and “More Whole” Forms of Unity Consciousness

As regards our ultimate nondual unity state, the idea, of course, would be to spend some time getting used to this, your Original Face, the nondual Suchness or Thusness or Isness of this and every moment. It is your own Primordial Being and Becoming, the groundless Ground and sourceless Source and goal-less Goal of evolution itself, the summum bonum of a human being—or any sentient being, for that matter. That pure nondual or unity state —especially its “Emptiness” component—can itself include a vast, even infinite number of items—especially in its “Form” component—and this includes things like, for example, the 6-to-8 structures (or Forms) of consciousness that we have already reviewed. And we will be adding a few other items that this unity consciousness can incorporate, increasing its “Form” or “Fullness” aspect, and thus becoming a unity that is actually more and more Whole. Remember, as we just saw, you can experience this unity state, this unity of Emptiness and Form, at virtually any of the 6-to-8 levels of development. But if I experience it at amber, I will indeed experience a direct unity with the entire world I am aware of—which means everything from the physical universe (atoms and molecules and stars and galaxies, etc.) to the biological universe (plants, reptiles, mammals, Gaia, etc.) to the mental universe (in this case, everything from infrared archaic to amber mythic, which is as far as I’ve developed at that point). But, as we noted earlier using “mythic Enlightenment” as an example, in the mental universe, if I am indeed at mythic, there are several worlds that are still “over my head” and that I therefore cannot be one with—I am not one with the orange rational world, or the green pluralistic world, or the turquoise integral world, and so on. But I will not directly experience this as a gap or a lack—I will not be aware of this at all. All I will experience is the pure unity of my world (without ever knowing that “my world” is actually more limited and narrower than it could be—that my “unity experience” could potentially be even deeper and more Whole).

So that is why we want to continue to learn about aspects of the manifest world of evolving Form—because the more of that world we are aware of, then the more Whole (and therefore more inclusive, more moral, more embracing, more expansive, with greater and greater depth) my “unity consciousness” will become, expanding into more and more of the world of Form with an ever-increasing Fullness, and thus likewise expanding the very nature of the union of Emptiness and Form—expanding Enlightenment itself.

And so, in that spirit of greater expansiveness and greater Wholeness and greater depth, we will be introducing, in the following chapters, a few more items (called “quadrants” and “lines”) that will, with our already included states and structures, add to this increase in expansiveness, add items that not only are of great interest in themselves, but will also directly feed our primary unity consciousness, our Original Face, our True Nature and Suchness, by including more and more of the world of Form and Fullness, the products of Spirit-in-action itself.

Whitehead used to speak of two fundamental aspects of Spirit: the Primordial Nature of Spirit (timeless and unchanging: our “Emptiness”) and the Consequent Nature of Spirit (the sum total of the results or products of Spirit-in-action or Eros or evolution, as they ceaselessly, with novelty and creativity, emerge in the ongoing stream of Becoming). This is not unlike the Eastern division of Spirit into, for example, nirguna Brahman (pure Spirit beyond all qualities, equivalent to “Emptiness”) and saguna Brahman (Spirit with qualities or characteristics, such as Being-Consciousness-Bliss, to which some would add Love, Creativity, the Good, the True, the Beautiful, and other qualities). The point, again, is that although the pure, unqualifiable, radical Freedom of Primordial Emptiness does not grow and evolve or move in time at all, Spirit’s manifest Forms most definitely grow, develop, and evolve, producing greater and greater Wholeness, moment to moment to moment—and the more of this Fullness we are aware of, the more of it we can become one with, deepening our unity Nature layer by layer by ever-more wholistic layer. This is a never-ending process, which means humans are on the path of an always fully Present, yet also never-ending, always-increasing path of unfolding Enlightenment. We are on a path without a goal, to a destination without date, on a quest without boundaries, with an awareness without limits, heading toward an ever-receding horizon of increasing Wholeness after Wholeness after Wholeness. This is the only life we will ever know, fully Complete Now, yet also Never-Ending. . . .

Great Perfection and Primordial Avoidance

So, as for this pure and complete One Taste in the timeless Now—rest in the vast, open, empty, clear oneness of pure unity consciousness, where everything that is arising in the entire universe is arising within you, within your headless, thoughtless, silent, still, pure, vast Awareness. If thoughts

arise, simply let them arise, as part of this total unity or Great Perfection. If your ego or separate self arises, simply let it arise, as part of this total unity or Great Perfection. If pain or suffering arises, simply let it arise, as part of this total unity or Great Perfection. If you think you understand this, then let that understanding arise, as part of this total unity or Great Perfection. If there are parts of this you don’t understand, let that arise as well, as part of this total unity or Great Perfection. Whether you understand this or don’t understand this, there is still this ever-present, Now-moment, headless nondual Awareness, arising of its own accord and spontaneously embracing the entire world. And so welcome, welcome home. It has always been so.

The world that we are aware of—in either the pure Witness state or the ultimate nondual Unity state—is a world where every single thing and event that arises is a perfect manifestation of the Divine, of Spirit, of the Great Perfection. It’s like a huge, vast Painting—the Total Painting of All That Is. The entire world around you—and everything within you—is fully part of this all-inclusive Total Painting of All That Is. Like any painting, there are light areas and dark areas, hills and valleys, highs and lows, brights and dulls, areas that are conventionally judged “good” and areas that appear “bad”— pleasure and pain, good and evil, better and worse, higher and lower—but the point is, all of them are necessary, are required, in order for the Total Painting to exist at all. If we got rid of all dark areas, shades, and shadows, the Painting would simply cease to exist—it would just be areas of pure white and all light, looking rather like a snowstorm in the Arctic, with no discernible features at all. Rather, every single thing and event that is arising is an intrinsic, necessary part of this great, vast, Total Painting of All That Is.

And the crucial point about that Total Painting is that it is also exactly the totality that we are to allow our awareness to fully be aware of. Whether we are neutrally reflecting or Witnessing All of It, or whether we are nondually one or united with All of It, the “All” part is the key. Our primordial Awareness is an all-embracing, all-encompassing, all-pervading Consciousness as Such, and it leaves out not a single thing or event arising in its Awareness Field. Whether we are totally Witnessing All of It, or are totally One with All of it, in either case, “All of It” (All of the Total Painting) is what our Awareness is meant to embrace, is what we are meant to be aware of, to prehend, to touch.

And if we watch closely, we will see that, in so many cases, there is some something—big or small, large or almost insignificant, but something—that

we don’t want to be aware of: something that we look away from, turn away from, and thus move away from. Some unpleasant body feeling; some uncomfortable thought; some difficult sight in our surroundings; some something that is just a little too unpleasant, too painful, too depressing, too anxiety-inducing, too close to suffering—and so we withdraw Awareness from that, however slightly—we look away, turn away, move away.

And in that single, simple first move lies the entire suffering of humanity. We move from heaven to hell in one step. We move from the utter Fullness and total Freedom of the Great Perfection of the Total Painting of All That Is, to an identity with an awareness that is an avoidance, that is a self-contraction, a contraction in the face of infinity—a narrow, finite, small, conventional, set-apart self—the self-contraction that is the source of literally all our suffering. Vast nondual Awareness is broken up into a finite subject versus some collection of finite objects. We have effectively lost the Fullness, the Totality, the Total Painting of All That Is, into which Awareness should be fully and completely relaxing. We should be letting Awareness arise and easily, impartially fall on All That Is, with no avoidances, no withdrawals, no contractions, no inward tension, no tortured escape mechanisms—but just a pure, wide, open, empty, clear, totally relaxed, totally embracing, all-pervading, noncontracting reflection of (or direct unity with) the Total Painting of All That Is: every single aspect of it as a Whole, the entire manifest universe in all its gorgeous glory, all its light and shade, high and low, pleasure and pain, ups and downs. This Total Relaxation—into All That Is—is your pure Seer and Real Self, your Original Face, your own pure I AMness (and, going even further, is the oneness of that Witnessing Self with the entire Total Painting itself, a Painting that is arising exactly where you thought your head was).

But Primordial Avoidance crashes that scene, severs that Field, divides and fragments it, judges it, separates it into what is good (deserving Awareness) and what is bad (deserving Avoidance). From this Total Field of spontaneously arising events, which our Awareness should be completely reflecting and easily embracing, we just pull back a bit, we vaguely look away a little, we merely contract a tad in the face of some unpleasantness— there’s just a little something that we’d rather not look at—and that Primordial Avoidance slams the world with a Primordial Boundary, dividing what is okay from what is not okay. With that, the entire scene crashes, smashes, and collapses around us, shattering into a million fragments that

will have to slowly be reassembled, piece by petty piece by pathetic piece. The whole clash of opposing opposites comes splintering onto the scene—

good versus bad, pleasure versus pain, desirable versus loathsome, wanted versus feared, grasped versus repressed, attractive versus abhorrent, “me” versus “not-me.” And our life becomes one of pursuing all the positive sides of the pairs of opposites and avoiding all of the negative sides—we want a life of all pleasure and no pain, all good and no bad, all loved and no feared. But trying to achieve a life of all pleasure, all good, all desirable, all lovable, is no more possible than a life of all rights and no lefts, all ups and no downs, all inside and no outside, all tops without any bottoms. The opposites are two sides of the same bloody coin. No wonder Vedanta Hinduism defines Enlightenment as being “freed of the pairs”—that is, freed from the pairs of opposites—from both opposites, not just the negative, but both of them together. Similarly, the Christian mystics refer to ultimate Reality as coincidentia oppositorium, “the unity of opposites.” But with the Primordial Avoidance—and that very first looking away, turning away, moving away— we begin to move, not in our unified Field of All That Is, but in a broken field of innumerable temporal opposites, seeking not their underlying unity but their ultimate separation—a life of all good and no bad, a life of all rich and never poor, always successful and never failing, all glorious and never wretched, all pleasure and never pain. Thus condemned to a pursuit of the impossible, our lives are a series of depressing disappointments and long stretches of utter boredom interrupted by spikes of abject terror.

And along with the Primordial Avoidance, there is likewise the birth of the instigator of the Avoidance—namely, the separate-self sense, the self-contraction. As the Upanishads also say, “Wherever there is other, there is fear.”13 But that’s what the self-contraction always sees—it’s a “subject” in here, aware of all sorts of objects, of “others,” out there. And these others it either frantically desires and grasps and pursues, or dreads and fears and avoids. This is the life of the self-contraction. And that life is built, not on Awareness, but on attention. “Awareness” is open, free, totally relaxed, inclusive of All that arises, and living in the timeless Now—Present to Present to Present. “Attention,” by contrast, is focused, contracted, never aware of the Total Painting but always narrowly directed to just a particular feature of it, living in the temporal present moving from the past to the future, unable to let go and fall into the ever-present Now, always constricting consciousness to the narrow-slit present existing in between the past and the

future, focused on one item at a time by one item at a time by one item at a time.

Ideally, of course, we should be capable of both: from an ever-present, never-abandoned Field of Awareness, we can focus attention on any aspect of the Total Painting of All That Is without losing touch with the All That Is or the Awareness disclosing it. But with Primordial Avoidance, we fracture that Field; we tear the Total Painting into those sections we want and like and desire, and those that we dislike, loathe, or fear—the world of opposed opposites comes crashing onto the scene, and our life becomes dedicated to the impossible, an impossible that does manage therefore to generate illusion, suffering, torment, and tears. Objects come, stay a bit, torture us, and move on, until finally we die—and that’s our life with Primordial Avoidance, the self-contraction, the world of “the pairs.”

Now, this Primordial Avoidance is pictured by most Traditions as some form of separation or dichotomy between subject and object—a separation that “destroys” the nonduality of the Field of pure Awareness (an illusory act, since nondual Awareness cannot be destroyed) and delivers it up apparently severed into self-versus-other, inside-versus-outside, heaven-versus-earth, infinite-versus-finite, nirvana-versus-samsara, Godhead-versus-creature. (To this subject-versus-object dichotomy, Integral Theory adds “individual-versus-collective,” giving the 4 quadrants—to be examined in the next chapter—which characterize all existing worlds.) Primordial Avoidance leads Spirit to successively “contract” and “reduce” itself, moving to ever denser and less real realms, moving (to use Christian terms) from Spirit to soul to mind to body to matter (with each of those still being Spirit, but an increasingly reduced version of Spirit: Spirit-as-spirit, Spirit-as-soul, Spirit-as-mind, Spirit-as-body, Spirit-as-matter), each successively possessing less consciousness, less awareness, less love, all the way down to the least aware, least loving, least conscious beings around, or material atomic and subatomic particles. At which point the Big Bang occurs and the purely material universe (with no life, no minds, no awakened souls) blows into existence.

This downward movement is known as involution (Plotinus called it “Efflux”). Once involution occurs, the upward movement of return to Spirit, known as evolution (Plotinus called it “Reflux”), can occur, and it does so in the basic reverse order of involution, evolving from insentient matter to living bodies to conceptual minds to awakened souls to pure Spirit-as-Spirit. Overall, this general “refluxing” or “evolving” movement, for the past 14

billion years, is about halfway home, having evolved matter, bodies, and minds, and standing now on the verge of introducing soul and then Spirit. (Although any individual can, at any point, take up a path of Waking Up and complete this refluxing movement in their own case, achieving a traditional Enlightenment or Awakening as they fully realize Spirit-as-Spirit in the deepest core of their own being.)

Now, the modern paths of Growing Up have added something to this picture, in addition to those major realms—which are actually versions of the 5 major natural states, with “matter” referring to the gross/physical realm, “body” to the beginning of interior states from feeling to illumination, “mind” to the causal root of consciousness per se, “soul” to Real Self or true Witness, and “Spirit” to nondual unity consciousness. Namely, in addition to those major realms (which were “preformed” and “sedimented” during involution), the return movement of evolution actually creates new realms or stages or levels as it itself unfolds and moves forward. In short, not only involution, but evolution, too, has a creative “Spirit-in-action” component, and thus each embodies a “creative advance” (with involution creatively becoming less and less, and evolution creatively becoming more and more). This advance is a creative act (of Eros or Spirit-in-action) that, by transcending and including each stage, evolutionarily creates more and more of the Total Painting of All That Is, until that Painting becomes conscious of, first, itself (with the emergence of reflexivity); then, of evolution (evolution becomes conscious of itself, or the fact that the Total Painting has evolved, and is continuing to do so); then of a 1st-tier evolution as it appears in humans (humans, too, are continuing to evolve or unfold, with an understanding of their earlier or 1st-tier stages); then of a 2nd-tier direct, self-transparent realization of evolution as it appears in humans (a self-reflexive realization of one’s own actual co-evolving with the Whole of the Kosmos); and then a 3rd-tier direct experience of evolution as Spirit-in-action via humans—a direct experience of Spirit, as Spirit, by Spirit, via humans.

The Traditions weren’t aware of most of the stages/levels of this part of creative evolution (such as the 6-to-8 levels of evolutionary development, spanning 1st, 2nd, and 3rd tiers), because they weren’t aware of structures or structure-stages themselves, and thus gave us only the Efflux/Reflux account as viewed through the lens of states and state-realms (gross, subtle, causal, witnessing, and original nondual—or matter, body, mind, soul, and spirit). But putting both of these sequences together (states and structures), we now

have a much more complete view of this overall movement of involution and evolution, Efflux and Reflux, outflow and inflow. And we realize that, unlike the way the Traditions imagined it, evolution is not merely a rewinding of the videotape of involution (with everything appearing in evolution having been previously laid down during involution, but “forgotten,” to be remembered as it all evolves in awareness). Rather, evolution is a mightily creative force in and of itself (a true example of Spirit-in-action), with most of its structures and stages being created as part of its own “creative advance into novelty,” a product not of a prior “otherworldly” involution but of an infinitely creative Eros (or Spirit-in-action) active in a “this-worldly” evolutionary drive. Thus, these levels of being and awareness do not have to be thought of as some “eternal ideas in the mind of God”14 (as premodern metaphysical systems everywhere usually picture them), but may be seen as creations of evolution itself as it constantly transcends and includes what it created in the previous moment. We don’t need “metaphysical” explanations for these creations (in the sense of eternally fixed ideas in the mind of an unchanging God); they are not “beyond” nature but “within” nature, the very interiors of the “this-worldly” evolution process itself. We generally refer to this as “integral post-metaphysics.”

But the central point for us is that, whether we view involution/evolution as being composed mostly of states/realms or structures/levels (or whether we include both of them, as Integral Theory does), in either case Primordial Avoidance is what sets the whole separative, involutionary, set-apartness, sedimenting, reducing, narrowing, contracting motion into play. We have fundamentally closed our eyes to something, something just a little too uncomfortable, and thus have primordially looked away, turned away, moved away—and that Primordial Avoidance has landed us with a self-contracting, finite, illusory “subjective” self (that is really an object), and a suffering-inducing, opposites-chasing denial of the Total Painting of All That Is. So violent was this “original sin” or “co-originating dualism” or “original alienation” that we have thrown ourselves into a real pit of despair, and have to dig ourselves out, at least pragmatically, via a series of pretend selves that become less and less divisive, less and less separative, less and less partial, and more and more whole, more and more unified, more and more conscious. This process culminates in the creation of a 3rd-tier, Super-Integral, Supermind realization that is the union of pure Emptiness (the pure Witnessing Self or Consciousness without an Object—“Absolute

Subjectivity”) with all Form, or all possible witnessed objects (gross, subtle, and causal), to produce the highest state (unity consciousness) in the highest structure. (That highest condition would be 3rd-tier Super-Integral or Supermind—or, at the least in today’s world, 2nd-tier Integral—but in any event it would be beyond the 1st-tier separate selves and their deficiency-driven needs.)

This “über-Realization” combines the highest of state realizations (the utter unity of Emptiness and Form, Godhead and creature, Infinite and Finite, nirvana and samsara) with the very highest reaches yet evolved of Form itself (Integral or Super-Integral). This guarantees that our “unity consciousness” is a real unity, a true Fullness, an utter Completeness—the comprehensive and full Total Painting of All That Is—which transcends and includes all levels and realms of being and awareness that evolution or Spirit-in-action has produced to date. There is nothing left “over our heads,” because we have no heads at all. “Over our heads” has turned into a big blue pancake and fallen on us—and we are it, no self-contraction, just unified One Taste.

What makes Supermind (the highest level in 3rd tier) different from Big Mind (nondual unity consciousness)15 is that Big Mind is a state; Supermind is a structure. Thus, Big Mind (Turiyatita) of Waking Up can be experienced at virtually any structure-stage of Growing Up—there is a mythic Big Mind experience, a rational Big Mind experience, a pluralistic Big Mind experience, and so on. (In each case, it is the experience of “the union of Emptiness and Form” at whatever level of Form development you are at— mythic, rational, pluralistic, etc. But less than Integral level is less than complete Form and Fullness—unity, yes, but less-than-complete unity.) In contrast, Supermind (the highest level in existence) can only be experienced after all the earlier, previous junior levels of consciousness and development have emerged and been laid down (i.e., all 1st- and 2nd-tier levels). Supermind transcends and includes all of those earlier structures, and thus when Supermind experiences Big Mind (which it invariably does—it comes with the territory at that high level; see below), at this highest level it is Big Mind as interpreted and experienced via every single major level of being and existence that has ever emerged in the entire history of evolution, all the way back to the Big Bang. Thus, Supermind = Big Mind + All Form that has evolved to date. This is what makes 3rd-tier Super-Integral Supermind (or even just ultimate 2nd-tier Integral mind) so extraordinary: it doesn’t just have access to the highest state yet evolved (turiyatita, Big Mind, nondual unity

consciousness); it also has access to every single major structure ever produced by evolution going all the way back to the Big Bang. This means its Emptiness is indeed the Freest of any Freedom possible anywhere, but its Form is the Fullest of any Fullness possible anywhere—the greatest Freedom with the highest Fullness: that is what 3rd-tier Supermind offers you (or close: ultimate 2nd-tier Integral mind).

(Big Mind is still the “union of Emptiness and Form,” but now, at this 2nd-tier—or better, 3rd-tier—level, Form is all Form that has emerged since the dawn of the universe with the Big Bang. This is why Supermind can’t be experienced until all levels of Growing Up have emerged and unfolded. You can be at, say, mythic or rational and have a “peak experience”16 of the state of Big Mind; but you cannot “peak-experience” Supermind at those levels. For that, rather, you have to evolve through not just the 1st- and 2nd-tier levels, but 3rd-tier levels as well, to their very highest—Supermind, which can then enter awareness fully and completely. In the meantime, simply staying at the Integral level, and practicing exercises such as Integral Meditation, will open you to such 3rd-tier realization—and until then, anyway, the Integral level will give us just about as much Wholeness as the Kosmos can contain at any stage anywhere. This is why we still continue to define the Integral structure/level as pragmatically the highest level of development yet to evolve in any substantial way anywhere; and yes, once you are clearly and fully at Integral, keep your eyes open for even higher Wholeness and Fullness and Freedom, as evolution continues its unrelenting march to ever-greater Inclusiveness.)

Just to say it one more time: the human being itself, from the start, contains (transcends and includes) every major holon or level of being and awareness ever to emerge, starting with the Big Bang. Humans literally contain, within their own being, quarks, subatomic particles, atoms, molecules, cells, and organ systems that include the basic biochemistry pioneered by plant life, the neural cord of fish and amphibians, the reptilian brain stem of reptiles, the limbic system of paleomammals (e.g., horses), the cortex of primates, and its own capping, complex triune brain with its neocortex—and from these (all of which are contained in the overall infrared Archaic level), humans continue to unfold, to transcend and include, through 6-to-8 levels of 1st-and 2nd-tier development, into the ultimate, furthest reaches of evolution to date: Super-Integral Supermind. Supermind, then—or, to repeat, at the least, 2nd-tier Integral for today’s actual evolution—is the holon with the most depth (and least span) in the entire Kosmos, the most significant (and least fundamental)

holon in the whole wide world, containing more levels of being than any other existing object or phenomenon anywhere in the known universe. Big Mind is simply a unified state (interpreted by whatever structure experiences it—mythic, rational, pluralistic, etc.); but Supermind is Big Mind seen and interpreted via the highest structure produced by all of evolution to date. This gives a staggering Fullness to Big Mind experienced by Super-Integral because the “unity of Emptiness and Form” here includes the standard Emptiness but whose Form is absolutely the Fullest Form ever to appear anywhere in the Kosmos. The Form component literally embraces and resonates with every level and every holon ever produced by evolution in any realm at all—all the way back to the very beginning of this universe. This adds a Depth to the Form of Super-Integral (or very close, Integral) that embraces the complete history of the Total Painting of All That Is as it has been continuously built up (transcended and included) day in and day out unstoppably for the past 14 billion years. There’s nothing like it anywhere else in the Kosmos, at any time. (And only the future will produce a yet greater Depth.)

Such is our True Self and Real Condition, or true nondual Suchness, in both its highest state and its highest structure. This is a simple, ever-present, pure Awareness of (or as) the Total Painting of All That Is, free of Primordial Avoidance and the self-contraction, and operating timelessly from ever-present Now to ever-present Now. This simple feeling of Being is already obvious to you right now—you are immediately aware of your pure I AMness, the simple feeling of Being right now, yes? This awareness is not hard to achieve, but impossible to avoid. The question is never how to reach it, but how to recognize its ever-always-present-ness. To that ever-present Big Mind experience, it is only necessary, while resting as headless Big Mind, to allow your own sequence of Growing Up to occur, taking you to yet Fuller and Fuller forms of unity consciousness on the way to Integral (or Super-Integral) levels of development, eventually transcending and including (while being fully one with) the entire universe—the Total Painting of All That Is—that evolution (or Spirit-in-action) has produced to date.

Let your Awareness arise in the vast Field of All Space. Let every single thing and event, every single aspect, of the Total Painting of All That Is arise easily, naturally, and fully in this open, clear, empty, spacious Awareness, and let it self-liberate into its own Suchness. Let there be no resistance, no contraction, no Primordial Avoidance of anything that arises. Like a great

Mirror-Mind fully accepting every reflection that arises on it—grasping nothing, rejecting nothing, accepting everything but sticking to nothing—so your own Awareness unhesitatingly allows all that arises to simply arise, reflects it fully, and lets it go. There is no looking away, no turning away, no moving away. The Fullness of the Total Painting fills your own Emptiness to infinity, an impartial Witnessing flipped into a prior, pure Unity or One Taste of All That Is—your own True Condition and Real Suchness.

The recommendations are thus simple and straightforward: In the Path of Growing Up, do as best you can to develop to Integral levels (thus on the very verge of Super-Integral). Using practices such as Integral Mindfulness, allow higher and higher levels of self and being and consciousness to continually come online, until, at Integral Wholeness, you stand face to face with the fullest version of the Total Painting of All That Is. And concomitantly, in the path of Waking Up, practice at least the states of pure Witnessing of this Total Painting, as well as headless at-one-ment with this Total Painting. Those combined realizations—highest structure with highest state—will evoke the very best and brightest from your own Unique being, and allow you to bring your deepest gifts to the world.

Even More . . .

Further, at those locations in the AQAL Matrix (“AQAL,” recall, is short for “all quadrants, all levels, all lines, all states, all types,” which are the elements to our composite or “super-map” of the human condition, which we have been using to orient us in our overall growth), you are literally at the very edge of evolution itself, and thus your very thoughts and actions are contributing directly to the Form or structure of tomorrow—you are a genuine co-creator of a reality that every human being henceforth will pass through. Make sure, therefore, that to the extent that you can, always act from the deepest, widest, highest source in you that you can find (such as Integral level and nondual state); let every word out of your mouth come from the Highest Self you can discern; let every action spring from the deepest Source you can possibly summon. You are laying down Forms that will be stored in that great storage bin in the Kosmos, whence they will one day reach down and mold the future with their own special insistence. Make sure those Forms will be something you can be deeply proud of. You do realize that you are directly co-creating a future World, don’t you? Please, never, never, forget

that. . . . Now, in the “return trip” that evolution has us on, there are several other

areas, other dimensions, other potentials that evolution itself has created in its constant “creative advance into novelty.” Being aware of those—bringing them into the Field of Awareness—can only potentiate and expand our genuine Condition, filling in yet more of its endless nooks and crannies, aspects of Spirit awaiting their remembrance, awaiting to be brought back on line, or perhaps waiting to emerge for the very first time, a novel expression of a novel evolution. Before fully signing off on our True Condition, let’s have a brief look at a few of these other aspects of the Total Painting of All That Is. For every one of these areas that is really there—and I believe they all are—their discovery and Awareness will only enhance and further rejuvenate the deep happiness of a Spirit rediscovered.

You are more than free to stop exactly where you are, with an understanding that you can Grow Up to Integral (and even Super-Integral) levels in your awareness, and you can Wake Up to Enlightenment or Liberation or the Great Awakening in your state consciousness—and adopt practices to keep both of those development sequences active and accelerated. If you do anything but that, you will be among the less than 1% of the worldwide population that has, at this time, developed to that depth in both sequences. This is, we might say, certainly enough for one lifetime.

But one of the things that you might have discovered as we went through the 6-to-8 major structure-stages of human development and the 4–5 major natural states of human awareness is how interesting these probably newly discovered areas were for you, and simply in themselves. Most people are fascinated by this new information, and immediately find it extremely useful in their lives. These other areas that we will introduce—although they are, in a sense, not quite as “important” as the areas we have already covered—are nonetheless, for most people, just as interesting, just as new, and just as useful in making their lives better, brighter, happier, healthier. These areas are also aspects of that “composite map” or “super-map,” and thus have been run past virtually every major human culture to appear in history— premodern to modern to postmodern to beginning integral—and thus they stand up to that “universal” test.

The next topic we will be exploring is called the “4 quadrants,” and a well-known trio derived from them, known as the Good, the True, and the Beautiful. You’ve likely heard of these Platonic ideas before (though almost

certainly not in the way we will present them). “The Good, the True, and the Beautiful” rings a bell in my own heart, even just hearing their names. Isn’t there some sort of intrinsic appeal to you as well? We can flesh out (or “Show Up” in) our Growing Up process as well as our Waking Up process by including the 4 quadrants in our own awareness. And just as with the stages of Growing Up and Waking Up, just hearing about these elements seems to activate them, to wake them up and get them online, to get them growing and developing in your own case, bringing all of their Goodness and all of their Truth and all of their Beauty straight into your own Being and Becoming, touching these dimensions with an Awareness that becomes more and more radiant, more dazzling, more alive and resonant, than ever before, just by psychoactively becoming aware of them, practicing Integral Mindfulness on them, bringing them into your own Awareness, thus becoming Fuller and Fuller and Fuller. Take this one further step, and I can virtually guarantee that this will indeed be a difference that makes a difference.

Not to mention the fact that once again you will have learned something truly crucial about our human condition. The elements of the AQAL Framework are like Human Being 101—they are the fundamental, crucial aspects of the human “being-in-the-world” that are so important that, in most cases, not a single major human culture in all of history has left them out. And yet, and yet . . . Cultures like ours effectively ignore or even repress much of this information—this absolutely fundamental information about what it means to be a human being—and thus leave individuals totally on their own to figure it out and find it out . . . if they ever do. You probably noticed that the two elements we have introduced so far—structures of Growing Up and states of Waking Up—are almost completely unmentioned, virtually unknown, in our culture at large. You were likely fairly surprised at seeing how much actual evidence there really is for their existence—not to mention how important they are. But why was this news to you? Versions of these truths should be part of every educational system—and common wisdom—in virtually every culture. We are, after all, talking about what it is that makes a human being a human being—it’s the instruction manual for how to use a human organism—and yet the whole thing is shoved under the carpet or hidden in the closet and left to luck to find it out. Well, luckily, you found this book—which is one version of that Human Being 101 information. And it includes the structures of Growing Up, and the states of Waking Up—

and the quadrants of Showing Up. Won’t you take a few more steps with me and see if the Good, the True, and the Beautiful speak deeply to you as well? This is, after all, part of that instruction manual of the universe and how to use it. . . .

Showing Up: The Many Perspectives of Consciousness

So far, we have looked at states and the path of Waking Up, and at structures and the path of Growing Up. In chapter 1 we also briefly examined shadow material and what might be called the path of Cleaning Up.

But before moving on to Showing Up, let me start by simply pointing out that, in this book, we are not going into the path of Cleaning Up in any great detail, and the reason is simple: the notions of “the unconscious” and “shadow material” are widely known and widely accepted. Since Freud’s immense contribution to psychology, several major schools of psychotherapy and psychiatry have developed, and the existence of “unconscious” or “repressed” material is largely agreed upon (at least by several major schools of psychology). Moreover, with little effort at all, you can find any number of local practitioners of various forms of psychotherapy in an area close to you, so that finding help in this topic is both fairly easy and widely accepted socially.

What I want to emphasize here—and the reason I am mentioning it at all— is that Cleaning Up is not handled well, or even at all, by any school of Growing Up or, importantly, any school of Waking Up. This means that if you are having emotional or mental problems, you will very likely have to address those issues separately. Simply taking up, say, a path of meditation will not, in and of itself, cure major and pressing shadow issues. These have to be directly approached, apart from other practices, and dealt with in any number of various ways (and research continues to show that the “cure” rate of these various treatments is essentially similar in all of the major and widely used forms of therapy—around 60% or so of significant improvement). Meditation can certainly help, and you might intentionally choose a therapist who works with meditation. But do not count on meditation by itself to handle your shadow issues. Spiritual approaches to psychotherapeutic problems, although they can help to some degree, just don’t address the major issues directly or effectively enough to be dependable and reliable. There are a large variety of approaches to shadow problems, and spending a little time online will show you just how large that number really

is. Simply realize that the major issue we are dealing with in this presentation

—finding out all of the major areas of your own being and awareness, and making them conscious—applies to hidden shadow material as well, and the approaches that we are covering—including Growing Up, Waking Up, and soon, Showing Up—are areas that are not widely known or accepted in our culture, and thus much of this material we are presenting will be quite new to you. But shadow work is, as noted, widely available and widely accepted, and has been for at least a hundred years now. I just wanted to point out that the general area of Cleaning Up is not well handled by Growing Up practices or Waking Up practices (nor Showing Up, either). I do, however, highly recommend taking up some form of therapy (including self-help versions widely available in books or online, which can be quite effective). Few people escape the tortures of being raised without some sort of shadow material, and that material is adroit at avoiding being an object of awareness —thus, it cannot be much helped by practices of Growing Up or Waking Up (which normally are also subjects that avoid becoming object), but it is skilled at derailing both of those paths to a significant degree if it is active and severe. So if you have any need of therapeutic work, definitely don’t hesitate to seek help.

Thus, although we won’t be focusing in detail on Cleaning Up, we are continuing the general theme of making subject object, of increasing our wholeness and unity-in-diversity, and of becoming increasingly aware of dimensions of our being and awareness that have been previously present but not fully, or even barely, realized. Thus, in addition to becoming aware of our structures of consciousness (in Growing Up) and our states of consciousness (in Waking Up), we’re going to give some serious attention to our perspectives in consciousness—a process we call “Showing Up,” or being authentically, fully Present in our lives. And I can fairly well assure you that, in just a few minutes, you will be discovering some new and fascinating dimensions of yourself.

So in this chapter, we’re going to look at the quadrants in connection to this general path of Showing Up. Just how many ways are there for us to actually Show Up in our lives?
